31560:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

31548:   function ppl(){return H.employees.filter(function(e){return String(e.role||'').toLowerCase()!=='owner'&&e.role!=='مالك'}).map(function(e){return{value:e.id,label:(e.name||e.email)+' — '+e.email}})}
31549:   async function loadPeople(){var d=await q('employees');H.employees=d.rows||[];return H.employees}
31550:   async function loadBranches(){var r=await supabase.from('branches').select('id,branch_code,name,is_active').eq('company_id',H.companyId).order('name');if(r.error)throw r.error;H.branches=r.data||[];return H.branches}
31551:   function branches(){return H.branches.filter(function(x){return x.is_active!==false}).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:(x.branch_code||'')+' — '+x.name}})}
31552:   function employeeOpts(){return ppl()}

31554:   function posOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.title}})}
31555:   function scheduleOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.name}})}
31556:   function tabbar(){return '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm p-2 flex gap-2 flex-wrap">'+T.map(function(x){return '<button type="button" data-hr-tab="'+x[0]+'" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-black text-sm '+(H.tab===x[0]?'bg-indigo-600 text-white':'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50')+'"><i class="fas '+x[2]+' ml-1"></i>'+x[1]+'</button>'}).join('')+'</div>'}
31557:   function employeeMeta(e){return '<div class="space-y-2 text-sm"><div><span class="text-slate-500">القسم:</span> <b>'+esc(e.department_name||e.department||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الوظيفة:</span> <b>'+esc(e.position_name||e.job_title||e.role||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الفرع:</span> <b>'+esc(e.branch_name||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">العقد:</span> '+(e.contract_status==='active'?badge('فعال','ok'):badge(e.contract_status||'غير موجود','muted'))+'</div></div>'}

31605:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){

31608:       e.preventDefault();

31610:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');

31612:     try{

31614:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');

31618:       });
31619:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
31620:     }catch(e){}
31621:   }());

29057: function _exportReportCsv() {
29058:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
29059: 
29060:     if (!resultDiv) {
29061:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للتصدير', 'info');
29062:         return;
29063:     }
29064: 
29065:     var table = resultDiv.querySelector('table');
29066: 
29067:     if (!table) {
29068:         _showToast('لا يوجد جدول قابل للتصدير', 'info');
29069:         return;
29070:     }
29071: 
29072:     var rows = table.querySelectorAll('tr');
29073:     var csv = [];
29074: 
29075:     function csvCell(value) {
29076:         var s = String(value == null ? '' : value)
29077:             .replace(/\r?\n|\r/g, ' ')
29078:             .replace(/"/g, '""');
29079: 
29080:         return '"' + s + '"';
29081:     }
29082: 
29083:     for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
29084:         var cells = rows[i].querySelectorAll('th,td');
29085:         var line = [];
29086: 
29087:         for (var j = 0; j < cells.length; j++) {
29088:             line.push(csvCell(cells[j].innerText || ''));
29089:         }
29090: 
29091:         csv.push(line.join(','));
29092:     }
29093: 
29094:     var blob = new Blob(
29095:         ['\uFEFF' + csv.join('\r\n')],
29096:         { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' }
29097:     );
29098: 
29099:     var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
29100:     var a = document.createElement('a');
29101: 
29102:     a.href = url;
29103:     a.download =
29104:         'rawaea-report-' +
29105:         String(_currentReport || 'report') +
29106:         '-' +
29107:         new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) +
29108:         '.csv';
29109: 
29110:     document.body.appendChild(a);
29111:     a.click();
29112:     document.body.removeChild(a);
29113: 
29114:     URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
29115: }
29116: function _printReport() {
29117:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
29118: 
29119:     if (!resultDiv || !resultDiv.innerHTML) {
29120:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للطباعة', 'info');
29121:         return;
29122:     }
29123: 
29124:     var printWindow = window.open('', '_blank');
29125: 
29126:     if (!printWindow) {
29127:         _showToast('الرجاء السماح بالنوافذ المنبثقة', 'warning');
29128:         return;
29129:     }
29130: 
29131:     var html =
29132:         '<!DOCTYPE html>' +
29133:         '<html dir="rtl">' +
29134:         '<head>' +
29135:         '<meta charset="UTF-8">' +
29136:         '<title>تقرير الروائع ERP</title>' +
29137:         '<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cairo:wght@400;700;900&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">' +
29138:         '<style>' +
29139:         'body{font-family:Cairo,sans-serif;padding:20px;color:#111827}' +
29140:         'table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:15px}' +
29141:         'th,td{border:1px solid #ddd;padding:8px}' +
29142:         'th{background:#f2f2f2;font-weight:800}' +
29143:         '.text-center{text-align:center}' +
29144:         '</style>' +
29145:         '</head>' +
29146:         '<body>' +
29147:         resultDiv.innerHTML +
29148:         '<script>window.onload=function(){window.print();};<\/script>' +
29149:         '</body></html>';
29150: 
29151:     printWindow.document.open();
29152:     printWindow.document.write(html);
29153:     printWindow.document.close();
29154: }

31496:   async function actor(){var a=await supabase.auth.getUser();if(a.error||!a.data.user)throw Error('جلسة المستخدم غير صالحة');var u=await supabase.from('users').select('id,email,company_id,role,name,status,phone,employee_id,default_branch_id,active_warehouse_role').eq('auth_id',a.data.user.id).maybeSingle();if(u.error)throw u.error;if(!u.data||!u.data.id||!u.data.company_id)throw Error('تعذر تحديد سياق الموظف والشركة');H.actor=u.data;H.companyId=u.data.company_id}

31595:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
31596:   (function installModalResilience(){
31597:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
31598:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
31599:       if(!ac)return;
31600:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
31601:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
31602:       e.preventDefault();
31603:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
31604:     },true);
31605:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
31606:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31607:       if(!root)return;
31608:       e.preventDefault();
31609:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
31610:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
31611:     });
31612:     try{
31613:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
31614:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31615:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
31616:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
31617:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
31618:       });
31619:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
31620:     }catch(e){}
31621:   }());

31644:             .replace(/</g, '&lt;')

31660:         var local = new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);

31629: var RW_CRM = (function() {
31630:     'use strict';
31631: 
31632:     var state = {
31633:         customers: [],
31634:         assignees: [],
31635:         kpi: {},
31636:         search: '',
31637:         activeOnly: false,
31638:         searchTimer: null
31639:     };
31640: 
31641:     function _esc(s) {
31642:         return String(s == null ? '' : s)
31643:             .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
31644:             .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
31645:             .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
31646:             .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
31647:             .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
31648:     }
31649: 
31650:     function _fmtNum(n) {
31651:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG');
31652:     }
31653: 
31654:     function _fmtMoney(n) {
31655:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG') + ' EGP';
31656:     }
31657: 
31658:     function _today() {
31659:         var d = new Date();
31660:         var local = new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);
31661:         return local.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
31662:     }
31663: 
31664:     function _statusLabel(s) {
31665:         var map = {
31666:             Open: 'مفتوحة',
31667:             'معلقة': 'معلقة',
31668:             completed: 'مكتملة',
31669:             'مكتملة': 'مكتملة',
31670:             cancelled: 'ملغاة',
31671:             'ملغاة': 'ملغاة'
31672:         };
31673:         return map[s] || s || 'غير محددة';
31674:     }
31675: 
31676:     function _statusClass(s) {
31677:         if (s === 'completed' || s === 'مكتملة') return 'bg-green-100 text-green-700';
31678:         if (s === 'cancelled' || s === 'ملغاة') return 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600';
31679:         return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700';
31680:     }
31681: 
31682:     function _assignedLabel(customer) {
31683:         var rows = Array.isArray(customer && customer.assigned_to) ? customer.assigned_to : [];
31684:         if (!rows.length) return 'غير مسند';
31685:         var active = rows.filter(function(x) { return x && x.active !== false; });
31686:         if (!active.length) active = rows;
31687:         return active.slice(0, 2).map(function(x) {
31688:             return x.name || x.email || '—';
31689:         }).join('، ') + (active.length > 2 ? ' +' + (active.length - 2) : '');
31690:     }
31691: 
31692:     async function _loadDirectory() {
31693:         var res = await supabase.rpc('crm_customer_directory', {
31694:             p_search: state.search || null,
31695:             p_active_only: state.activeOnly,
31696:             p_limit: 200,
31697:             p_offset: 0
31698:         });
31699:         if (res.error) throw res.error;
31700: 
31701:         var payload = res.data || {};
31702:         state.customers = Array.isArray(payload.customers) ? payload.customers : [];
31703:         state.assignees = Array.isArray(payload.assignees) ? payload.assignees : [];
31704:         state.kpi = payload.kpi || {};
31705:         return payload;
31706:     }
31707: 
31708:     function _renderKpis() {
31709:         var k = state.kpi || {};
31710:         return '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4">' +
31711:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">إجمالي العملاء</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.total_customers) + '</div></div>' +
31712:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">عملاء نشطون</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-green-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.active_customers) + '</div></div>' +
31713:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">ذمم مسجلة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-red-600 mt-2">' + _fmtMoney(k.master_debt_total) + '</div></div>' +
31714:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متابعات مفتوحة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-amber-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.open_followups) + '</div></div>' +
31715:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متأخرة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-rose-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.overdue_followups) + '</div></div>' +
31716:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">مستحقة اليوم</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-blue-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.due_today) + '</div></div>' +
31717:         '</div>';
31718:     }
31719: 
31720:     function _renderTable() {
31721:         if (!state.customers.length) {
31722:             return '<div class="text-center py-16 text-gray-400"><div class="text-5xl mb-3">👥</div><div class="font-black text-lg">لا توجد عملاء مطابقون</div><div class="text-sm mt-2">غيّر البحث أو الفلاتر ثم أعد المحاولة.</div></div>';
31723:         }
31724: 
31725:         var html = '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-sm">' +
31726:             '<thead class="bg-slate-50"><tr>' +
31727:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">العميل</th>' +
31728:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التواصل</th>' +
31729:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التصنيف</th>' +
31730:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المبيعات</th>' +
31731:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الأوردرات</th>' +
31732:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المتابعة القادمة</th>' +
31733:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">المسؤول</th>' +
31734:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الإجراء</th>' +
31735:             '</tr></thead><tbody>';
31736: 
31737:         for (var i = 0; i < state.customers.length; i++) {
31738:             var c = state.customers[i] || {};
31739:             var overdue = Number(c.overdue_followups || 0) > 0;
31740:             var next = c.next_followup_date ? String(c.next_followup_date) : '—';
31741: 
31742:             html += '<tr class="border-b hover:bg-slate-50">' +
31743:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-black">' + _esc(c.name) + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.customer_code) + '</div></td>' +
31744:                 '<td class="p-3"><div>' + _esc(c.phone || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.area || '—') + '</div></td>' +
31745:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-bold">' + _esc(c.customer_type || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.payment_type || '—') + '</div></td>' +
31746:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtMoney(c.sales_total) + '</td>' +
31747:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtNum(c.order_count) + '</td>' +
31748:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black ' + (overdue ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700') + '">' + _esc(next) + '</span></td>' +
31749:                 '<td class="p-3">' + _esc(_assignedLabel(c)) + '</td>' +
31750:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><button type="button" data-crm-open360="' + _esc(c.id) + '" class="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl font-black">متابعة</button></td>' +
31751:             '</tr>';
31752:         }
31753: 
31754:         return html + '</tbody></table></div>';
31755:     }
31756: 
31757:     function _bindDirectory() {
31758:         var search = byId('crm-search');
31759:         if (search) {
31760:             search.value = state.search;
31761:             search.addEventListener('input', function() {
31762:                 state.search = search.value.trim();
31763:                 clearTimeout(state.searchTimer);
31764:                 state.searchTimer = setTimeout(function() {
31765:                     _loadDirectory().then(function() {
31766:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-customers-list'), _renderTable());
31767:                         _bindOpen360();
31768:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-kpis'), _renderKpis());
31769:                     }).catch(function(e) {
31770:                         showToast(e.message || 'فشل البحث', 'error');
31771:                     });
31772:                 }, 250);
31773:             });
31774:         }

31595:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
31596:   (function installModalResilience(){
31597:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
31598:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
31599:       if(!ac)return;
31600:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
31601:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
31602:       e.preventDefault();
31603:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
31604:     },true);
31605:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
31606:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31607:       if(!root)return;
31608:       e.preventDefault();
31609:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
31610:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
31611:     });
31612:     try{
31613:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
31614:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31615:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
31616:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
31617:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
31618:       });
31619:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
31620:     }catch(e){}
31621:   }());
